{"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Abstractly, additive number theory includes the study of abelian groups and commutative semigroups with an addition operation.","type":"example"},{"text":"Two principal objects of study in additive number theory are the sumset A#43;B#61;#92;#123;a#43;b#58;a#92;inA,b#92;inB#92;#125; of two subsets A and B of elements from an abelian group G, and the h-fold sumset of A, hA#61;#92;underset#123;h#125;#123;#92;underbrace#123;A#43;#92;cdots#43;A","type":"example"},{"text":"Additive number theory has close ties to combinatorial number theory and the geometry of numbers.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The subfield of number theory concerning the behaviour of sumsets (integer sets added to each other elementwise)."],"tags":["uncountable"],"topics":["mathematics","number-theory","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":8064376,"upstream_offset":18967472333,"upstream_record_sha256":"8b1c75cc2d89742d9a332f187d5f74e84d958a02ed99d4e9d20b530cb75e93e2","word":"additive number theory"}
